																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Just as everyone has different tastes in music and movies, the works of art that move us are deeply personal. Some find comfort in clean lines and structured compositions, while others are drawn to bold colors or dreamlike, mysterious imagery. The moment you discover your own artistic taste among countless works, the way you experience space begins to change.</p>



<p>Samsung Art Store brings together everything from classical masterpieces to contemporary works from world-renowned museums, galleries and artists, all on a single screen. It allows you to build a personal gallery tailored to your shifting tastes through monthly themed curations and collections from leading global art fairs.</p>



<p>So, which artistic movement best reflects your taste? Take a simple test below to discover your signature artistic style, and find the perfect Samsung Art Store collections to bring a fresh new atmosphere into your everyday space.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and Broadcom Expand Strategic Collaboration Across Memory and Foundry Technologies]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-and-broadcom-expand-strategic-collaboration-across-memory-and-foundry-technologies</link>
				<pubDate>Sat, 25 Jul 2026 14:32:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/25142854/Samsung-Semiconductors-Memory-and-Foundry-Technologies-Broadcom-MoU_Thumb932_F-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Semiconductors]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Broadcom]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[HBM]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[MoU]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/3RgydPH</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and Broadcom today announced the signing of a memorandum of understanding (MOU) to expand their strategic collaboration across memory and foundry technologies, supporting the next generation of AI infrastructure. The MOU was announced during the AI Summit, held at The Midway in San Francisco. Attendees included Jinman Han, President and Head of Samsung […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics and Broadcom today announced the signing of a memorandum of understanding (MOU) to expand their strategic collaboration across memory and foundry technologies, supporting the next generation of AI infrastructure.</p>



<p>The MOU was announced during the AI Summit, held at The Midway in San Francisco. Attendees included Jinman Han, President and Head of Samsung Electronics’ Foundry Business, Hock Tan, President and CEO of Broadcom, senior executives from both companies and representatives of the Korean government.</p>



<p>The companies expect the collaboration to be estimated at more than $200 billion across memory and foundry over the next five years through 2030.</p>



<p>On the memory front, Samsung and Broadcom plan to pursue a strategic collaboration for the supply of industry-leading memory solutions, including High Bandwidth Memory (HBM), supporting Broadcom’s next-generation AI accelerators.</p>



<p>In foundry, the companies’ collaboration focuses on Samsung’s 2-nanometer (nm) and below process technologies for Broadcom’s products, including Wireless Broadband Communications (WBC) solutions. The collaboration is expected to extend to advanced packaging technologies built on Samsung’s 2nm process, including 2.3D and 2.5D integration, to enable higher-performance and more power-efficient AI and networking silicon.</p>



<p>“AI is driving unprecedented demand for tightly integrated semiconductor technologies spanning memory, logic and advanced packaging,” said Young Hyun Jun, Vice Chairman & CEO of the Device Solutions (DS) Division at Samsung Electronics. “By expanding our collaboration with Broadcom across these critical technologies, we look forward to delivering greater value to customers while advancing the AI infrastructure of the future.”</p>



<p>“As AI infrastructure continues to scale, close collaboration across the semiconductor ecosystem becomes increasingly important,” said Charlie Kawwas, President, Semiconductor Solutions Group of Broadcom. “By combining Samsung’s memory and foundry expertise with Broadcom’s AI and connectivity leadership, we aim to continue to deliver technologies that power the next generation of AI infrastructure.”</p>



<p>With capabilities encompassing memory, logic, foundry and advanced packaging, Samsung is uniquely positioned to deliver integrated semiconductor solutions for the AI era. The expanded collaboration with Broadcom reflects Samsung’s continued focus on supporting customers with end-to-end semiconductor technologies across an increasingly diverse range of AI and high-performance computing applications.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026] Samsung and Partners Envision a Connected Care Future — From First Signal to Lasting Change]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/galaxy-unpacked-july-2026-samsung-and-partners-envision-a-connected-care-future-from-first-signal-to-lasting-change</link>
				<pubDate>Thu, 23 Jul 2026 16:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23144743/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Health]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4pCRG9U</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics set out its vision for the next chapter of digital health — a future where care moves from reactive treatment toward preventive, personalized and connected experiences, supported by trusted health innovation and partnerships across the healthcare ecosystem. To explore that vision, Samsung hosted a Health Forum […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics set out its vision for the next chapter of digital health — a future where care moves from reactive treatment toward preventive, personalized and connected experiences, supported by trusted health innovation and partnerships across the healthcare ecosystem.</p>



<p>To explore that vision, Samsung hosted a Health Forum panel, “Rewriting the Rules of Preventive Health.” Moderated by Dr. Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics, the session brought together leaders across nutrition, fitness, clinical research and AI to examine how technology can close the gap between knowing and doing in everyday health.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23145616/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main1.jpg" alt="(From left) Otávio Penatti, Kevin Duffy, Moderator Dr. Hon Pak. Rohit Ail and Kartik Poria" class="wp-image-175876" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Otávio Penatti, Kevin Duffy, Moderator Dr. Hon Pak. Rohit Ail and Kartik Poria</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>The Paradox — and the “Missing Miles” of Health</strong></h2>



<p>Opening the discussion, Dr. Pak described a paradox at the heart of modern health: innovation and technology today has been transformational and accelerating, yet chronic disease continues to rise around the world.</p>



<p>“We live in the most measured era in human history — billions of people track their sleep, their stress, their heart rate every single day. And yet chronic disease keeps rising. More data, it turns out, hasn’t resulted in better health.”</p>



<p>He framed the disconnect as two “missing miles.” The first mile is detection and monitoring: much of what shapes our health happens outside the clinic and until recently, continuous monitoring was neither available nor connected to the physician. The last mile is action: even after a diagnosis and a care plan, it is the individual who has to act day after day — and life is busy and change is hard.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23145641/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main2.jpg" alt="Samsung, Dr. Pak argued, is uniquely positioned to close both gaps." class="wp-image-175877" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Dr. Hon Pak</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung, Dr. Pak argued, is uniquely positioned to close both gaps. With technology that stays with people throughout their daily lives — across wearables, mobile phones and a connected ecosystem — Samsung can understand not just raw biometric numbers, but the context behind them.</p>



<p>“We don’t just see a number — we see what’s around it: whether this is a moment you’re ready to change something or a moment you need a bit of support. Being there, at the point where personal decisions actually get made, is hard for a hospital or any other company to measure or act upon.”</p>



<p>But no single company can do this alone. That, he said, is why Samsung is building Connected Care together with partners — closing the first and last mile to scale prevention, with AI as the enabler across both. He introduced the three focus areas that would structure the conversation: everyday behavior, clinical credibility and privacy and advanced AI and research.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Everyday Behavior: Closing the Last Mile at the Dinner Table and the Gym</strong></h2>



<p>Diet and exercise are two of the biggest levers on long-term health — and two of the most within our control. They are also, Dr. Pak noted, where the gap between knowing and doing is widest.</p>



<p>Kartik Poria, Head of Product at Samsung Food, located that gap in a moment everyone recognizes.</p>



<p>“It’s Tuesday, six o’clock. You’re tired, you’re staring into the fridge and everything you know about eating well loses to whatever’s quickest. That moment is what we’re going after — because what you eat, day after day, is one of the biggest levers we have on preventing chronic disease.”</p>



<p>He described how Samsung Health and Samsung Food are coming together to tackle two halves of the same problem: Samsung Health surfaces what is happening beneath the surface — indicators such as meal logs, BMI, the Antioxidant Index and the AGEs Index — while Samsung Food’s global recipe library facilitates meal planning and its food intelligence reflect how people actually like to eat. Sitting on top is a nutrition experience grounded in behavior change science rather than tracking alone — learning a person’s habits and working with them so the healthier pattern becomes the easier one.</p>



<p>“We’re not adding another thing to manage — we’re making the healthy choice the easy one, for the night you’ve got the energy to cook and the night you don’t.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23145830/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main3.jpg" alt="Kartik Poria, Head of Product at Samsung Food, located that gap in a moment everyone recognizes." class="wp-image-175880" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Kartik Poria from Samsung Food</figcaption></figure>



<p>Turning to movement, Kevin Duffy, CEO of iFIT, described exercise as foundational to both physical and mental wellbeing and explained how iFIT’s expert-led programs are becoming more accessible through Samsung Health.</p>



<p>“Exercise is vital for both physical and mental wellbeing — cardiovascular health, strength, mobility, sleep quality, stress management.”</p>



<p>Through Samsung Health, users can discover and subscribe to iFIT’s premium fitness programs, while iFIT workouts integrate with Galaxy Watch so users can track key metrics during guided sessions. With Fitness Index, they receive a holistic view of their physical fitness in a single, easy-to-understand score, with personalized recommendations. Looking ahead, Duffy pointed to Samsung’s Health Assistant as a way to bring a user’s Fitness Index, goals and activity history together to surface the most relevant workout at the right moment.</p>



<p>“Exercise is not just about being active — it’s about understanding your body, building sustainable habits and having the right guidance at the right time. By combining iFIT’s expert-led experiences with Samsung Health’s connected insights, we can help more people make movement a meaningful part of their everyday lives.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="751" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23145858/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main4.jpg" alt="Turning to movement, Kevin Duffy, CEO of iFIT, described exercise as foundational to both physical and mental wellbeing." class="wp-image-175881" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Kevin Duffy from iFIT</figcaption></figure>



<p>But content and insights alone, Dr. Pak noted, don’t close the last mile. The harder question is how to move from engagement to activation. Samsung designs for real life, recognizing that people slip and miss goals most of the time — and that those moments are precisely when timely, well-contextualized support matters most.</p>



<p>The real measure, he argued, is not how often the system nudges someone, but how many healthy actions actually happen — a doctor’s low-salt advice quietly showing up as items in a grocery cart or a workout that meets a person where they actually are that day. Poria added that this is the true shift: from nudging people to changing the choice in front of them.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Clinical Credibility and Privacy: Trust Earned Through Evidence</strong></h2>



<p>Dr. Pak turned next to the question of trust — noting that wearable data is now something most clinicians have seen, but far fewer have been able to build into their day-to-day care. Closing that gap, he said, is a matter of evidence, workflow and trust.</p>



<p>Rohit Ail, Head of the Technology Incubation Lab at Samsung R&D Institute UK (SRUK), spoke to how his team works to generate clinical evidence in real-world settings.</p>



<p>“Our goal is to meet the standards expected by clinicians, researchers and regulatory bodies and that means generating evidence in real-world clinical environments. This commitment builds on Samsung’s growing body of clinical research and validation evidence.”</p>



<p>He referenced ongoing collaborations across Europe, including partnerships with the NHS and clinical validation studies with leading hospitals. With San Cecilio University Hospital in Spain and Gemelli in Italy, Samsung is working on identifying and managing cardiovascular disease (CVD) risk using AI and decision-support technologies; with Fondazione IRCCS in Italy, the focus is on understanding and addressing the late effects faced by adolescent and young adult (AYA) cancer survivors through AI-based digital phenotyping and a non-invasive, holistic approach.</p>



<p>On privacy, Ail noted that Samsung considers user data to belong to the user, with Samsung acting as custodian.</p>



<p>“Trust begins with protecting users’ data. Samsung takes a three-layer approach: Samsung Knox for device-level security, GDPR-compliant infrastructure and alignment with the European Health Data Space framework.”</p>



<p>He added that SRUK has also developed a Decentralized Clinical Trial framework, in which participant identities remain under the control of healthcare organizations and data is pseudonymized from the outset.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23145939/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main5.jpg" alt="Rohit Ail, Head of the Technology Incubation Lab at Samsung R&D Institute UK (SRUK), spoke to how his team works to generate clinical evidence in real-world settings." class="wp-image-175882" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Rohit Ail from Samsung R&D Institute UK</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Advanced AI and Research: Reading the Body’s Signals</strong></h2>



<p>If everyday reach and clinical trust are two of the three pillars, intelligence is what binds them together. Dr. Pak introduced foundation models as a genuine turning point — a shift from population averages to something truly personal.</p>



<p>Otávio Penatti, Head of the Health AI R&D Team at Samsung R&D Institute Brazil (SRBR), explained what a foundation model for health actually means.</p>



<p>“A health foundation model is an AI model that uses self-supervised learning to learn meaningful features from unlabeled biosignal data. At Samsung, we are advancing health foundation model research across multiple global research teams, combining expertise in biosignal intelligence, wearable AI and personalized health insights. Building on the success of large language models, we can now interpret complex biosignals — such as ECG, ACC and PPG — not as isolated data points, but as a structured biological language. These signals can be measured through wearable devices in everyday situations and by applying foundation models to understand these biological patterns, we have the potential to provide more accurate predictions and continuous, personalized health insights.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/23150017/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Health-Forum-Sketch_main6.jpg" alt="Otávio Penatti, Head of the Health AI R&D Team at Samsung R&D Institute Brazil (SRBR), explained what a foundation model for health actually means." class="wp-image-175883" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Otávio Penatti from Samsung R&D Institute Brazil</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Three Focus Areas, One Goal</strong></h2>



<p>To close, Dr. Pak asked each panelist for a one-sentence vision of Connected Care in the era of AI five years from now.<strong></strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Kartik Poria (Samsung Food):</strong> “In five years, eating well won’t run on willpower — it’ll feel like having a chef and a dietitian in your pocket, turning what your body needs into what’s for dinner, day after day.”</li>



<li><strong>Kevin Duffy (iFIT):</strong> “A coach that knows your body and adapts every workout to who you are that day.”</li>



<li><strong>Rohit Ail (SRUK):</strong> “Care that clinicians trust and patients control — evidence-based and private by design.”</li>



<li><strong>Otávio Penatti (SRBR):</strong> “AI that understands the body’s language and acts before problems begin.”</li>
</ul>



<p>Dr. Pak closed by tying the threads together: everyday behavior, clinical trust and personal AI — three focus areas, one goal of closing the first and last mile to scale prevention while meeting people where they are. “The future of health is connected, trusted and proactive — and it’s a road we’re glad to be traveling together.”</p>
]]></content:encoded>

									<description><![CDATA[At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled the eighth-generation Galaxy Z series. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled the eighth-generation Galaxy Z series. Combining agentic AI that understands users with hardware tailored to different lifestyles, the lineup offers three distinct foldable experiences.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom got an early look at the new devices during Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 — from Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, the first Ultra model in the Galaxy foldable portfolio, to Galaxy Z Fold8 with its all-new form factor and Galaxy Z Flip8 with more intuitive AI on FlexWindow.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="496" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220302/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main1.jpg" alt="(From left) Galaxy Z Flip8, Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra" class="wp-image-175788" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Galaxy Z Flip8, Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra: The Ultra Experience on a Foldable Display</strong></h2>



<p>Within the Galaxy lineup, the “Ultra” name represents Samsung’s highest level of performance and user experience. As the first Ultra model in the Galaxy Z series, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra pairs ultra-level performance with an expansive foldable display to elevate productivity through AI-powered multitasking.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="564" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220341/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main2.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a remarkably thin profile when unfolded." class="wp-image-175789" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a remarkably thin profile when unfolded.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The first thing that stands out is how thin the device is. Unfolding it reveals a spacious 8-inch main display. Measuring 4.1 millimeters thick when unfolded and weighing only 215 grams, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra is the thinnest Galaxy Z Fold yet — making it comfortable to carry and use throughout the day.</p>



<p>The display has also been refined to deliver smoother, more comfortable folding. The folding magnets, hinge mechanism and display tension have been precisely optimized, while refinements to the edge design make the device easier to unfold. Samsung’s proprietary Flex Titanium technology helps minimize the display crease while enhancing durability.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="725" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220359/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main3.jpg" alt="Flex Titanium technology reduces crease visibility." class="wp-image-175790" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Flex Titanium technology reduces crease visibility.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The expansive display also unlocks enhanced AI experiences.</p>



<p>Gemini Notebook streamlines the process of organizing meeting materials and notes. Users can upload a PDF of meeting minutes or an audio recording of a presentation to generate a summary of the key points, then transform the same source material into a variety of formats — including AI-generated videos, presentation slides, podcasts and quizzes.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="747" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220433/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main4.jpg" alt="Gemini Notebook streamlines the process of organizing meeting materials and notes." class="wp-image-175791" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Gemini Notebook</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra truly shines in AI-powered multitasking. Agentic AI anticipates when multitasking is needed, using Now Nudge to open Multi Window at the right moment. For example, Now Nudge can suggest adding dinner plans from a messaging app to the calendar and open the Calendar app in Multi Window.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="450" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220448/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main5.jpg" alt="Agentic AI anticipates when multitasking is needed, using Now Nudge to open Multi Window at the right moment." class="wp-image-175792" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Now Nudge</figcaption></figure>



<p>Qualcomm Snapdragon® 8 Elite Gen 5 for Galaxy and a 5,000mAh battery support Galaxy AI experiences and seamless multitasking on the expansive display.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Fold8: A Foldable, Reimagined</strong></h2>



<p>Redesigned with an all-new form factor, Galaxy Z Fold8 introduces an aspect ratio optimized for a wide range of content.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220505/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main6.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8 features new display proportions across its 5.5-inch cover screen and a 7.6-inch main display." class="wp-image-175793" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8 features new display proportions across its 5.5-inch cover screen and a 7.6-inch main display.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Unfolding the device expands the display to a 4:3 aspect ratio, ideal for watching movies or playing games. Rotating the device switches to a 3:4 aspect ratio that feels more natural for reading e-books or browsing the web. The cover screen’s 10:16 aspect ratio also provides a familiar experience for browsing short-form content.</p>



<p>A 4,800mAh battery supports more than 26 hours of video playback. Weighing just 201 grams, Galaxy Z Fold8 is the lightest Galaxy Z Fold yet and remains comfortable to hold — even during extended viewing sessions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220556/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main7.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Optimized aspect ratios enhance content immersion.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 goes beyond content consumption with tools for creating and editing.</p>



<p>Galaxy AI-powered My FanCam makes it easy to create videos focused on a specific person. After selecting a video in the Gallery app, users can launch My FanCam and choose the subject to follow. The video is then automatically reframed around the selected subject.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220901/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main8.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam</figcaption></figure>



<p>The device also delivers a more advanced agentic AI experience.</p>



<p>For example, if a user shares a photo of the Eiffel Tower with Gemini and asks it to book a nearby hotel, the AI agent finds accommodations that match the specified criteria — even while they continue using other apps. Afterward, they can simply review the recommendations and approve only the actions that require confirmation, saving time and effort.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="387" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220923/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main9.jpg" alt="For example, if a user shares a photo of the Eiffel Tower with Gemini and asks it to book a nearby hotel, the AI agent finds accommodations that match the specified criteria." class="wp-image-175798" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Agentic AI</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Flip8: More Without Unfolding</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8 makes a strong first impression with its thin, lightweight design. Measuring 6.1 millimeters thick when unfolded and weighing only 180 grams, it offers excellent portability. A stronger frame and a more durable hinge made with a new high-strength material improve durability while maintaining the device’s slim profile.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="726" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220943/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main10.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Flip8 is comfortable to hold." class="wp-image-175800" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Flip8 is comfortable to hold.</figcaption></figure>



<p>One of Galaxy Z Flip8’s biggest upgrades is its more versatile FlexWindow. Users can check important information and access their favorite features without unfolding the device.</p>



<p>Another highlight is Galaxy AI’s Now Brief. From FlexWindow, users can instantly see the day’s weather, schedule and important notifications. Everyday information such as exchange rates and stock updates is organized into personalized cards, making it easy to stay on top of the day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="467" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22220958/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main11.jpg" alt="From FlexWindow, users can instantly see the day’s weather, schedule and important notifications." class="wp-image-175801" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Now Brief on FlexWindow</figcaption></figure>



<p>App functionality also extends to FlexWindow. Without unfolding the device, users can browse photos in the Gallery app, chat with friends through messaging apps and quickly complete tasks. More than a hub for notifications and widgets, FlexWindow has evolved into a gateway to more of what Galaxy has to offer.</p>



<p>The camera experience has also been refined to better complement the Galaxy Z Flip form factor. Camcorder Grip — exclusive to the Galaxy Z Flip series — enables stable one-handed recording, while Super Steady’s horizontal lock helps keep footage smooth and level, even on the move.</p>



<p>Dual Recording captures both the creator and the subject in the same video, perfect for vlogs and social media challenges. Opening the Camera app displays the creator’s view at the top of the screen and the subject’s view below, allowing both perspectives to be recorded simultaneously. A live preview is also available on the cover screen, so both can see the shot while filming.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="649" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22221014/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-A-First-Look_main12.jpg" alt="Dual Recording captures both the creator and the subject in the same video, perfect for vlogs and social media challenges." class="wp-image-175802" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Dual Recording</figcaption></figure>



<p>From AI-powered productivity on a large display to optimized content viewing and everyday convenience without unfolding, the eighth-generation Galaxy Z series expands the possibilities of foldables. Designed to suit a wide range of needs, the latest Galaxy foldables offer users more ways to choose the mobile experience that suits them.</p>

									<description><![CDATA[At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, expanding the digital health experience on the wrist. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, expanding the digital health experience on the wrist. From the moment users wake up to the time they finish a workout and begin recovery, a smartwatch stays in contact with the body throughout the day.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom got an early look at the new wearables during Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 — from Galaxy Watch Ultra2, built for precise workout tracking and structured training in demanding environments, to Galaxy Watch9, designed for everyday health management spanning sleep, cardiovascular health, exercise and recovery.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="693" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214454/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main1.jpg" alt="(From left) Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 " class="wp-image-175743" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 </figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2: Adventure Without Limits</strong></h2>



<p>Combining top-tier performance with exceptional durability, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is optimized for high-intensity sports and outdoor activities.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is the first Galaxy Watch powered by the Qualcomm Snapdragon® Wear Elite Platform,<sup data-fn="c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710" class="fn"><a href="#c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710" id="c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710-link">1</a></sup> delivering fast performance and precise GPS tracking. Its 800mAh battery offers 35% more capacity than the previous generation’s. The display reaches up to 5,000 nits of brightness — a first for a smartwatch — making on-screen information easy to read even under bright lighting.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214540/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main2.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is available in a 47-millimeter size. " class="wp-image-175745" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is available in a 47-millimeter size. </figcaption></figure>



<p>Comfort has also improved. The cushion design seamlessly integrates the display, bezel and body. Despite its larger battery, the watch is 12% thinner than the previous generation thanks to component optimization and an internal redesign. The band is 26% lighter with a new air pocket structure, providing a secure yet comfortable fit.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="519" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214607/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main3.jpg" alt="A slim profile provides lasting comfort. " class="wp-image-175746" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ A slim profile provides lasting comfort. </figcaption></figure>



<p>Trail Run, a new feature exclusive to Galaxy Watch Ultra2, offers real-time insights including detailed elevation data, climbing progress and cumulative ascent and descent distance. With this information, users can gauge workout intensity and adjust their pace accordingly.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="467" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214621/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main4.jpg" alt="Trail Run " class="wp-image-175747" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Trail Run </figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2’s capabilities now extend beneath the surface. From the moment users enter the water, it automatically records depth, dive time and water temperature. Backed by EN13319 certification for diving instruments, along with 10ATM water resistance and an IP69K rating for dust and water resistance, the device is engineered to support safe underwater activities. Furthermore, in collaboration with global diving equipment brand Mares, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 will support advanced diving features — including real-time ascent and descent rates as well as safety decompression time.<sup data-fn="3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76" class="fn"><a href="#3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76" id="3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>During workouts, users can take advantage of hydration guidance, newly added to Nutrition Alert. Based on sweat loss relative to body weight, it calculates recommended fluid intake in real time to help users stay hydrated during extended exercise sessions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214635/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main5.jpg" alt="Hydration guidance " class="wp-image-175749" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Hydration guidance </figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch9: A Companion for Everyday Health</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Watch9 is designed to be an everyday companion for continuous health management. Its slimmer body and band with a new air pocket structure fit comfortably and securely around the wrist, making it ideal for all-day wear — from workouts to sleep.</p>



<p>Powered by the Snapdragon Wear Elite platform, Galaxy Watch9 features a 390mAh battery<sup data-fn="8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5" class="fn"><a href="#8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5" id="8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5-link">3</a></sup> with 20% more capacity than the previous generation’s to support fast performance and consistent health monitoring throughout the day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="707" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214646/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main6.jpg" alt="(From left) Galaxy Watch9 comes in 40- and 44-millimeter sizes. " class="wp-image-175753" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Galaxy Watch9 comes in 40- and 44-millimeter sizes. </figcaption></figure>



<p>The Galaxy Watch series’ digital health experience is centered around the updated Samsung Health, an all-in-one health coach that brings together five core pillars — Activity, Mindfulness, Nutrition, Sleep and Vitals — in one place. By capturing signals from the body and presenting them at a glance, the app helps users stay ahead of their health.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214718/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main7.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The five core pillars of Samsung Health </figcaption></figure>



<p>Sleep is the foundation of health management. The upgraded Sleep Apnea feature analyzes the number of breathing disruptions per hour using an AI algorithm and categorizes them into three levels. It further refines the sleep apnea detection capability that earned Samsung the world’s first De Novo authorization from the U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) in 2024.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214732/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main8.jpg" alt="Sleep insights " class="wp-image-175755" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Sleep insights </figcaption></figure>



<p>Key health metrics measured during sleep can be viewed at a glance in Vitals. Using heart rate, heart rate variability, respiratory rate, skin temperature, blood oxygen saturation, Galaxy Watch9 establishes a personalized baseline to help identify meaningful changes in health.</p>



<p>Heart Health Score provides an easy-to-understand snapshot of cardiovascular health by combining factors such as activity level, sleep, body composition and stress trends into a single score representing the user’s current condition.</p>



<p>Workout plans can also be tailored to fitness levels. Fitness Index uses body composition and workout history to assess whether a user’s current exercise volume is appropriate, helping set individualized goals. On days with more intensive workouts, Daily Cardio Load helps users assess training balance and determine how much recovery time they need.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214756/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main9.jpg" alt="Heart Health Score " class="wp-image-175756" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Heart Health Score </figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 excels in high-intensity training, while Galaxy Watch9 supports everyday health management. Together with enhanced hardware and Samsung Health, the next-generation Galaxy Watch series delivers a smarter, more personalized health experience.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p><strong>Samsung Electronics</strong> today unveiled the new Galaxy Z series, its most complete lineup yet, designed to expand foldables to a wider audience. The lineup introduces Galaxy Z Fold8, a fresh and exciting form factor that provides a new kind of foldable experience built around the way people explore, discover and immerse themselves in their favorite content.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22215435/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main1_F.jpg" alt="Samsung Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 open a new chapter in foldables with three distinct experiences designed for Ultra productivity, immersive discovery and self-expression." class="wp-image-175769" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 open a new chapter in foldables with three distinct experiences designed for Ultra productivity, immersive discovery and self-expression.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The lineup also includes Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, which brings Galaxy’s renowned Ultra standard to foldables with maximum productivity and content creation in a larger workspace. Galaxy Z Flip8 — the sleekest one yet — was built for expression and quick interaction on the go. Built on seven generations of foldable innovation and consumer insight, the new Galaxy Z series gives users more choice than ever, with three distinct experiences engineered to fit different needs.</p>



<p>Galaxy AI has been optimized for each of these form factors. Across the lineup, every insight feels more actionable and every action feels more connected. Multitasking is easier on the larger screens of Galaxy Z Fold8 and Fold8 Ultra, while Flip8’s AI-native FlexWindow keeps relevant information and shortcuts within reach. And with partner AI experiences such as Gemini Intelligence, Galaxy gives users even more ways to get things done in fewer steps — with transparency, privacy and user control built in.</p>



<p>“As AI becomes more agentic, mobile devices will become the most personal entry point to experiences that understand and adapt to each user,” said TM Roh, Chief Executive Officer, President and Head of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “By setting a new standard for foldables, we are once again advancing the premium mobile experience and opening the next era of intelligence to more people.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>The All-New Galaxy Z Fold8: A New Galaxy Fold Experience for Everyday Immersion</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 is reimagined for users who want a foldable that feels natural in the moments when curiosity turns into deeper discovery. Fold8 introduces a form factor designed to move easily from quick interactions on the cover screen to deeper viewing, reading and gaming on the main display. With intuitive display ratios and long-lasting performance, Fold8 makes it easier to get the most out of the content, stories and ideas that matter.</p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Built for Discovery</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8’s display ratios are designed around the way people naturally consume content throughout the day. By adapting the screen to the content, Fold8 creates a more immersive experience whether users are browsing, watching, reading or gaming. At just 201 g,<sup data-fn="6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708" class="fn"><a href="#6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708" id="6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708-link">1</a></sup> Fold8 is Samsung’s lightest Galaxy Z Fold yet.<sup data-fn="84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d" class="fn"><a href="#84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d" id="84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d-link">2</a></sup> Despite its lightweight design, it combines the Snapdragon® 8 Elite Gen 5 Mobile Platform for Galaxy<sup data-fn="b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa" class="fn"><a href="#b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa" id="b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa-link">3</a></sup> with a larger 4,800 mAh battery,<sup data-fn="27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53" class="fn"><a href="#27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53" id="27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53-link">4</a></sup> delivering smooth, responsive performance and lasting power so users can stay immersed longer.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="369" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230343/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main2_FINAL.jpg" alt="Samsung’s new Flex Titanium technology introduces a titanium-based display structure, combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, to make foldables thinner while maintaining durability." class="wp-image-175854" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung’s new Flex Titanium technology introduces a titanium-based display structure, combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, to make foldables thinner while maintaining durability.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>When folded, the <strong>10:16 cover screen</strong><sup data-fn="b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896" class="fn"><a href="#b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896" id="b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896-link">5</a></sup> feels familiar and comfortable for the short moments people return to most often, such as messaging, social updates, browsing and short-form video.</li>



<li>When unfolded, the <strong>4:3 main display</strong><sup data-fn="502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811" class="fn"><a href="#502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811" id="502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811-link">6</a></sup> creates a larger canvas that helps users lose themselves in games and films.</li>



<li>When rotated, the same display ratio becomes naturally suited to reading, giving users a more comfortable way to settle into articles, e-books and long-form content without carrying another device.</li>



<li>Together, these display ratios are designed around the way people naturally consume content, helping Fold8 feel more immersive whether users are quickly checking in or settling in for longer sessions.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>A Display Engineered From the Inside Out</strong></h4>



<p>Across the new Galaxy Z series, Samsung’s display engineering expertise serves as the foundation for a more refined foldable experience, supporting slimmer designs, greater durability and more seamless viewing.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Samsung’s new <strong>Flex Titanium</strong> technology introduces a titanium-based display structure designed to help make foldables thinner while maintaining durability. Combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, Flex Titanium helps strengthen display support, absorb pressure and impact and reduce crease visibility over time. This structure also refines the way the device opens, balancing hinge mechanics, display tension and magnetic force to make unfolding feel smoother, lighter and more natural.<sup data-fn="85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd" class="fn"><a href="#85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd" id="85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd-link">7</a></sup></li>



<li>With up to <strong>3,000 nits</strong> of brightness, <strong>Vision Booster</strong> and a <strong>low-reflection display finish</strong>, the main screen stays vivid and easy to see even in outdoor settings.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>A Clear and Versatile Camera Experience</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 is designed to help users capture and share the moments that catch their attention. Whether it’s a sweeping landscape, a group shot or a spontaneous moment on the move, Fold8 makes everyday photography and video feel easier to capture, edit and share.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="372" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230405/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main3_FINAL.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8’s dual 50 MP cameras deliver consistent high-resolution detail across wide and ultra-wide perspectives." class="wp-image-175855" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8’s dual 50 MP cameras deliver consistent high-resolution detail across wide and ultra-wide perspectives.</figcaption></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22221111/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main4.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8’s dual 50 MP cameras deliver consistent high-resolution detail across wide and ultra-wide perspectives." class="wp-image-175805" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8’s dual 50 MP cameras deliver consistent high-resolution detail across wide and ultra-wide perspectives.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Dual 50 MP cameras</strong> deliver consistent high-resolution detail across wide and ultra-wide perspectives, helping preserve image quality across different scenes and shooting conditions.<sup data-fn="914d2760-4047-4a67-98fb-7c2875328de2" class="fn"><a href="#914d2760-4047-4a67-98fb-7c2875328de2" id="914d2760-4047-4a67-98fb-7c2875328de2-link">8</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Dual Recording</strong> lets users capture both sides of the moment while previewing themselves through the cover screen, making reactions and shared experiences easier to frame.</li>



<li><strong>My FanCam</strong><sup data-fn="5a55293b-9d33-4b64-b5d4-d7746af8f4a0" class="fn"><a href="#5a55293b-9d33-4b64-b5d4-d7746af8f4a0" id="5a55293b-9d33-4b64-b5d4-d7746af8f4a0-link">9</a></sup> automatically tracks a selected subject and reframes footage into a preferred aspect ratio, turning dynamic scenes into social-ready videos with less manual editing.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="556" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230438/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main5_FINAL.jpg" alt="My FanCam automatically tracks a selected subject and reframes footage into a preferred aspect ratio, turning dynamic scenes into social-ready videos with less manual editing." class="wp-image-175856" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ My FanCam automatically tracks a selected subject and reframes footage into a preferred aspect ratio, turning dynamic scenes into social-ready videos with less manual editing.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra:</strong> <strong>The Ultra Foldable Built for Maximum Productivity</strong></h2>



<p>Ultra represents Galaxy’s highest standard for performance, capability and innovation. With Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, that standard meets Samsung’s most advanced foldable design. Its expansive 8-inch main display<sup data-fn="53c03bba-a828-40bf-978b-3e4f0ede72fc" class="fn"><a href="#53c03bba-a828-40bf-978b-3e4f0ede72fc" id="53c03bba-a828-40bf-978b-3e4f0ede72fc-link">10</a></sup> opens up a productive workspace for multitasking, while its advanced camera system and long-lasting battery help users create throughout the day. All of this comes in Galaxy’s slimmest Z Fold design yet — measuring just 4.1 mm when unfolded and weighing 215 g,<sup data-fn="10c2821e-3edc-46f8-9fb9-70a00f8774ca" class="fn"><a href="#10c2821e-3edc-46f8-9fb9-70a00f8774ca" id="10c2821e-3edc-46f8-9fb9-70a00f8774ca-link">11</a></sup> it pushes the standard for productivity on the go.</p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Professional-Grade Creativity With an Ultra-Level Camera System</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra is built to give creators more freedom from capture to edit, with an Ultra-level camera system and creative tools that make high-quality content easier to produce from one device.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="396" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230525/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main6_FINAL.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra is built to give creators more freedom from capture to edit, with an Ultra-level camera system and creative tools that make high-quality content easier to produce from one device." class="wp-image-175857" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra is built to give creators more freedom from capture to edit, with an Ultra-level camera system and creative tools that make high-quality content easier to produce from one device.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The <strong>200 MP main camera</strong> system captures rich, detailed photos with greater clarity, and is now enhanced with <strong>High Dynamic Range (HDR)</strong> capability in 200 MP mode for more true-to-life images.<sup data-fn="5468e458-930d-4de9-9043-ac71cd5cbb2b" class="fn"><a href="#5468e458-930d-4de9-9043-ac71cd5cbb2b" id="5468e458-930d-4de9-9043-ac71cd5cbb2b-link">12</a></sup></li>



<li>A new <strong>50 MP ultra-wide camera</strong> brings greater detail and clarity to wider scenes and macro shots, giving users more flexibility when capturing landscapes, interiors, group shots and close-up details.</li>



<li>Improved <strong>Nightography</strong><sup data-fn="10fdb4e3-a8b1-4091-98e1-d23db48c8f0a" class="fn"><a href="#10fdb4e3-a8b1-4091-98e1-d23db48c8f0a" id="10fdb4e3-a8b1-4091-98e1-d23db48c8f0a-link">13</a></sup> helps photos and videos look brighter and clearer in low light, so users can capture standout moments even in challenging lighting conditions.</li>



<li>For advanced creators, <strong>8K video recording</strong> through the new <strong>APV codec</strong> gives users greater flexibility and quality for editing and production workflows, while <strong>Cine LUT</strong> adds cinematic color and tone control directly from a mobile device.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Power, Battery and Cooling Built for All-Day Productivity</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra is engineered to stay fast, responsive and reliable through the most demanding workloads, from heavier multitasking and content creation to AI-powered workflows.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>With <strong>Snapdragon</strong>®<strong> 8 Elite Gen 5 Mobile Platform for Galaxy</strong><sup data-fn="595861fc-23da-40ea-805b-b2188c368b61" class="fn"><a href="#595861fc-23da-40ea-805b-b2188c368b61" id="595861fc-23da-40ea-805b-b2188c368b61-link">14</a></sup> at its core, Fold8 Ultra delivers the performance users need to switch between apps, work across a larger screen and keep demanding tasks moving smoothly.</li>
</ul>



<p><strong>A larger 5,000 mAh battery</strong><sup data-fn="6bb4256c-c231-40a0-9f1c-8ce5e5d082a3" class="fn"><a href="#6bb4256c-c231-40a0-9f1c-8ce5e5d082a3" id="6bb4256c-c231-40a0-9f1c-8ce5e5d082a3-link">15</a></sup> helps users stay productive for longer,<sup data-fn="c6895238-6a89-4054-a813-c3cc38666205" class="fn"><a href="#c6895238-6a89-4054-a813-c3cc38666205" id="c6895238-6a89-4054-a813-c3cc38666205-link">16</a></sup> supporting a full day of work, creation and entertainment in a slimmer foldable design.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large"><img loading="lazy" width="1024" height="683" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22221254/10.Samsung-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra_battery-1024x683.png" alt="Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra’s larger 5,000 mAh battery helps users stay productive for longer, supporting a full day of work, creation and entertainment in a slimmer foldable design." class="wp-image-175808" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra’s larger 5,000 mAh battery helps users stay productive for longer, supporting a full day of work, creation and entertainment in a slimmer foldable design.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Fold8 Ultra introduces a <strong>new dual-path charging architecture</strong> with 45W fast charging, helping distribute power more efficiently across the battery system for faster, more stable and reliable charging throughout the day.</li>



<li>Fold8 Ultra uses an expanded graphite cooling structure engineered for improved thermal conductivity, helping dissipate heat more effectively to keep the device responsive during demanding tasks.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Z Flip8: The Sleekest, Most Expressive AI-Native Flip Experience</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8 is built for users who want their phone to feel as dynamic as they are. Designed around quick interactions, personal expression and AI-native experiences, Flip8 brings the power of Galaxy AI closer to everyday moments through a reimagined FlexWindow and uniquely flexible Flip form factor. Weighing just 180 g<sup data-fn="cf25c2ff-d4f2-4454-b996-d069b78f95d4" class="fn"><a href="#cf25c2ff-d4f2-4454-b996-d069b78f95d4" id="cf25c2ff-d4f2-4454-b996-d069b78f95d4-link">17</a></sup> and with a thickness of only 6.1 mm, Samsung’s slimmest and lightest Flip<sup data-fn="5c946d05-abb6-46e8-95b5-62ab714aee26" class="fn"><a href="#5c946d05-abb6-46e8-95b5-62ab714aee26" id="5c946d05-abb6-46e8-95b5-62ab714aee26-link">18</a></sup> brings information, actions and creativity closer to everyday moments.</p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>A Smarter, More Intuitive FlexWindow Experience</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8 reimagines the FlexWindow as an AI-native everyday interface, bringing information, actions and assistance closer to the moments when users need them most.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22221333/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main8.jpg" alt="The Galaxy Z Flip8’s new FlexWindow displays insights and next steps through Now Brief, helping users move from glanceable information to action at the right moment." class="wp-image-175809" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The Galaxy Z Flip8’s new FlexWindow displays insights and next steps through Now Brief, helping users move from glanceable information to action at the right moment.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The new <strong>FlexWindow</strong> brings apps, insights and actions directly to the cover screen, helping users access information, AI-powered features and connected tasks in fewer steps.</li>



<li><strong>Now Brief</strong><sup data-fn="7dfbdfcd-747f-42f4-929f-b61555d4b673" class="fn"><a href="#7dfbdfcd-747f-42f4-929f-b61555d4b673" id="7dfbdfcd-747f-42f4-929f-b61555d4b673-link">19</a></sup> on the FlexWindow home screen brings timely insights and next steps, helping users move from glanceable information to action at the right moment.</li>



<li>Flip8 also makes automation<sup data-fn="f11e17ae-555b-4cb5-a42a-7ab41fc32cb4" class="fn"><a href="#f11e17ae-555b-4cb5-a42a-7ab41fc32cb4" id="f11e17ae-555b-4cb5-a42a-7ab41fc32cb4-link">20</a></sup> through <strong>Gemini Intelligence</strong> easier to access from the FlexWindow,<sup data-fn="3f3f7af7-ee10-4501-99c9-465c3b5eba4b" class="fn"><a href="#3f3f7af7-ee10-4501-99c9-465c3b5eba4b" id="3f3f7af7-ee10-4501-99c9-465c3b5eba4b-link">21</a></sup> allowing users to complete connected actions through side-key activation or natural voice requests.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>FlexCam Unlocks a More Expressive Flip Selfie Experience</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Flip8 enhances Samsung’s signature Flip camera experience, turning everyday moments into more expressive content through flexible shooting angles, real-time previews and Flip-only creative experiences. From hands-free shooting and real-time preview to cover-screen selfie videos and Flip-only mirror selfies, Flip8 helps users create, check and share polished moments without setting up extra equipment.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22221406/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main9.jpg" alt="From hands-free shooting and real-time preview to cover-screen selfie videos and Flip-only mirror selfies, Galaxy Z Flip8 helps users create, check and share polished moments without setting up extra equipment." class="wp-image-175811" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ From hands-free shooting and real-time preview to cover-screen selfie videos and Flip-only mirror selfies, Galaxy Z Flip8 helps users create, check and share polished moments without setting up extra equipment.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>A <strong>50 MP camera with</strong> <strong>ProVisual Engine</strong> delivers Galaxy Z Flip’s most advanced selfie experience, helping users capture detailed portraits and everyday shots with natural skin tones, dynamic bokeh and standout detail.<sup data-fn="1b3de725-3aa1-412f-be70-7b03265fa365" class="fn"><a href="#1b3de725-3aa1-412f-be70-7b03265fa365" id="1b3de725-3aa1-412f-be70-7b03265fa365-link">22</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Flex Mode</strong> makes hands-free shooting easy from a wide range of angles, while <strong>Camcorder Grip </strong><strong>with Zoom Rocker </strong>provides a more comfortable, stable hold when capturing dynamic shots on the move.</li>



<li><strong>Super Steady </strong>— now with a <strong>Horizontal Lock </strong>option — helps keep footage smooth and consistently framed, whether filming action-filled moments or on-the-go selfie videos.</li>



<li><strong>FlipSho</strong><strong>t </strong>lets users personalize the FlexWindow and capture more expressive mirror selfies, while <strong>Mirror view</strong> gives users a realistic mirror-like view for quick look checks before taking a photo or heading out.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A New Class of Agentic Experiences Optimized for Foldables</strong></h2>



<p>As the most personal device people carry, the phone has become the primary entry point where AI meets users — and the new Galaxy Z series sets the tone for the next generation of mobile AI experiences. Galaxy AI now works across more apps and services, with experiences optimized for foldable form factors. By understanding what matters to each user and helping turn insights into action, Galaxy AI becomes more personal, proactive and useful throughout the day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22225622/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main10_FINAL-1.mp4"></video></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Now Brief</strong> helps users stay informed with personalized insights tailored to their routines, interests and daily needs through expanded scenarios, customizable briefing cards and personalized recommendations. It now also surfaces security and privacy insights, helping users stay on top of potential risks and protective actions.</li>



<li><strong>Now Nudge</strong><sup data-fn="89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f" class="fn"><a href="#89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f" id="89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f-link">23</a></sup> helps turn insights into action. When plans begin to take shape in a conversation, AI can surface relevant next steps such as checking a schedule, finding a place or saving a location. On foldables, users can continue the conversation side by side on larger displays.</li>



<li><strong>Gemini Intelligence</strong> helps users move from request to result with fewer steps. Users can simply describe what they need or even add screen or image context. App automation can now work across more than 40 supported apps and services, with capabilities ranging from looking up tickets to finding a nearby restaurant and making a reservation. With <strong>Gemini Notebook</strong>, users can bring together notes, images, recordings, files and documents in one persistent workspace to collect, organize and develop their ideas. Drag-and-drop support in Split view makes the workflow even more intuitive, allowing users to easily add multiple files and images and turn their content into audio overviews, infographics, reports, meeting recaps, visual summaries, documents and more.</li>



<li>AI experiences become more connected and contextual when the new Galaxy Z series is connected with<strong> Galaxy Watch Ultra2, Galaxy Watch9</strong> and even the upcoming <strong>intelligent eyewear</strong>. From wellness insights to daily routines and connected actions, Galaxy AI helps users move more seamlessly between devices with timely personalized support throughout the day.</li>
</ul>



<p>Together, these experiences show how Galaxy AI is becoming a more capable and proactive companion, helping users accomplish more with less effort. Across the lineup, foldable form factors make these experiences easier to access throughout the day.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Security and Privacy Built Into the Experience</strong></h2>



<p>As AI becomes more personal and agentic, trust continues to be a critical foundation of the experience. Users need to know how their data is used, understand what AI is doing on their behalf and stay in control of the choices that shape their experience. With the new Galaxy Z series, Samsung builds that trust into the device from the start, keeping personal data secure while giving users transparency, choice and control over how their information is used.</p>



<p>Samsung Knox protects every Galaxy device from the chip up, with a multi-layered approach that includes on-device personalization and user-controlled cloud processing, as well as future-ready protections such as ecosystem-wide protection through Samsung Knox Matrix. For on-device AI, Personal Data Engine (PDE) enables context-aware, personalized AI experiences. To protect PDE, <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-inside-the-tech-powering-safe-personalized-galaxy-ai-experiences" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Knox Enhanced Encrypted Protection (KEEP)</a> creates encrypted, app-specific storage environments, while <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-how-galaxy-ai-protects-privacy-with-samsung-knox-vault" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Knox Vault</a> adds a tamper-resistant physical layer for sensitive information.<sup data-fn="ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c" class="fn"><a href="#ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c" id="ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c-link">24</a></sup> These system-level protections work together with Advanced Intelligence settings and Personal Data Intelligence to help users <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-how-galaxy-ai-empowers-you-to-take-control-of-your-data" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">control how AI features manage and use personal data</a>.</p>



<p>One UI 9 also introduces a new AI Assistant Activity dashboard, giving users a centralized view of AI automations running on their behalf. Instead of moving across multiple apps and services to understand what happened, users can review AI actions in one place and navigate directly to the relevant agent<sup data-fn="67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6" class="fn"><a href="#67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6" id="67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6-link">25</a></sup> for additional context and settings. The new Galaxy Z series also brings enhanced Privacy Alerts that proactively notify users about potential privacy risks, including unnecessary attempts to access background permissions, helping them respond before those risks become invisible.</p>



<p>These updates build on Galaxy’s existing security and privacy portfolio which includes Auto Blocker, Theft Protection, Private Sharing, Secure Wi-Fi and more. Together, these advancements help users take a more active role in managing personal data and AI activities, with transparency, clearer choices and stronger protections at the core of their experience — helping ensure that more personal AI experiences remain grounded in trust.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Getting Started With the New Galaxy Z Series</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 are available for pre-order in select markets starting today.  All models are available in Graphite and Cream, with Violet Shadow exclusive to Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Lavender to Galaxy Z Fold8 and Pink to Galaxy Z Flip8. Online-exclusive colors<sup data-fn="8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3" class="fn"><a href="#8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3" id="8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3-link">26</a></sup> <sup data-fn="ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24" class="fn"><a href="#ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24" id="ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24-link">27</a></sup> include Green Shadow for Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Pistachio for Galaxy Z Fold8 and Mint for Galaxy Z Flip8.</p>



<p>The new Galaxy Z series will bring the benefits of advanced AI closer to users’ daily lives by providing a 6-month free trial of Google AI Pro,<sup data-fn="e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d" class="fn"><a href="#e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d" id="e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d-link">28</a></sup> including 5 TB of cloud storage — equivalent to $19.99 USD per month. With the expanded access to Google’s most capable AI models, users can boost their productivity and creativity on the foldable screen anytime they want.</p>



<p>Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever, thanks to a more seamless migration experience across a wider range of devices.<sup data-fn="9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e" class="fn"><a href="#9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e" id="9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e-link">29</a></sup> With the updated Smart Switch, iOS users can scan a QR code on their current devices and wirelessly transfer data without downloading additional apps. Smart Switch can also bring over additional types of data including passwords and passkeys, call history, accessibility settings, eSIM data and more, helping reduce the time needed to set up a new device.  And with Quick Share’s new compatibility with AirDrop,<sup data-fn="22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047" class="fn"><a href="#22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047" id="22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047-link">30</a></sup> sharing files between iOS and Galaxy devices is now seamless in both directions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="494" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230609/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main11_FINAL.jpg" alt="Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever with the updated Smart Switch, allowing iOS users to scan a QR code and wirelessly transfer data without additional apps." class="wp-image-175858" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever with the updated Smart Switch, allowing iOS users to scan a QR code and wirelessly transfer data without additional apps.</figcaption></figure>



<p>To give users peace of mind after upgrading, Samsung Care+<sup data-fn="e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701" class="fn"><a href="#e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701" id="e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701-link">31</a></sup> offers comprehensive protection with fast repairs for accidental damage, extended warranty coverage and certified expert support at home and abroad, making it easier to stay covered wherever life takes them. Customers who purchase a new Galaxy foldable on Samsung.com and enroll in a monthly Samsung Care+ plan will receive the first three months of coverage at no cost. With One UI 9, the new Warranty and care settings hub also makes it easy to enroll in, access and manage Samsung Care+, alongside warranty information, self-diagnostics, repair requests and remote support — all from one convenient place on the device.</p>



<p>For more information about the new Galaxy Z series, please visit: <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Global Newsroom</a> or <a href="http://www.samsung.com/galaxy-z-fold8/?referrer=newsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung.com.</a></p>

KEEP is available on Galaxy smartphones and tablets with One UI 8 or later. <a href="#ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 24"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6">AI Assistant Activity provides visibility into eligible agent activities. Availability of agents and activities may vary by market, model and OS version. <a href="#67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 25"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3">Exclusively available on Samsung.com. <a href="#8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 26"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24">Color availability may vary depending on country or carrier. <a href="#ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 27"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d">Gemini is a trademark of Google LLC.
